# Marks by floor

7166 marked rows over 5976 distinct tiles on 6 floors. Generated by `level_map.py`; re-run after every sweep.

A row can carry several registers. `persona_other`, `telegraphic`, `garbled`, `institution` and `doomsayer` come from `labels.classify()` unchanged; everything else is a regex overlay, so it over-reports and every entry carries its quote. `question_deflection`, `non_english` and `vendor_swap` were added after floor 0 (FINDINGS 89); the first is a claim about the prompt rather than the self. Rows whose answer correctly names itself an AI are listed under `self_as_ai` rather than under `persona_other`.
